Discover Japan
A cinematic Japan tourism landing page designed to showcase destinations, seasonal experiences, and travel packages through an editorial-inspired visual experience.
Live Website
Visit Discover Japan
Overview
Discover Japan is a tourism landing page concept built to help visitors explore Japan's regions, discover experiences for different seasons, and learn about travel packages.
Highlights
- Cinematic, editorial-inspired Japan travel presentation
- Destination and regional highlights
- Seasonal experience discovery
- Travel package showcase
- Enquiry form UI
- Responsive landing-page layout and visual animations
Project Status
The website is deployed on Higgsfield. The enquiry form is currently a demo interface; submissions are not connected to a production email or database unless separately configured.
Links
- Live site: https://discover-japan.higgsfield.app
- GitHub repository: Add your repository URL here after creating the repository.
Disclaimer
This project is a tourism landing-page concept. Destination details, package information, and enquiry functionality should be verified and configured before using the site for real bookings.

    Press space again to drop the item in its new position, or press escape to cancel.

user selected Create goal. Create a saved goal with a plan for their request. Use the request_user_input tool for setup questions so the user can answer in the question UI. Ask up to three focused questions together: the first check-in date and time if missing, and only essential details needed to shape the goal. Offer concrete choices and allow a custom answer. A trip date or goal deadline is not a check-in time. Wait for the answers, then create the goal using the automations tool with a nonempty plan and the agreed schedule before doing detailed planning. Keep remaining optional details as open questions in the plan; do not require another round of planning questions before saving. Do not invent a check-in time or add recurrence without agreement. If saving fails, explain the error and ask only for what is needed to fix it. Confirm only after saving succeeds, and update the same goal in later replies instead of creating duplicates. Keep implementation details and tool names out of user-facing replies.\"},{\"connectors\":{\"connector_openai_olympic\":{\"template\":\"internal://olympic\",\"host\":\"health_onboarding\",\"widgetType\":\"olympic\",\"viewParams\":{\"view\":\"onboarding_v3\"}}}},{\"background_asset\":\"rosalind-helix\",\"acknowledgement_button_text\":\"Get started\",\"title\":\"Rosalind Workbench\",\"subtitle\":\"Perform scientific research with frontier reasoning models and scientific tools in one place\",\"sections\":[{\"subtitle\":\"Reason across biology\",\"text\":\"Explore molecules, proteins, genes, pathways, and disease biology in one reasoning flow.\",\"icon\":\"brain\"},{\"subtitle\":\"Work with scientific tools\",\"text\":\"Connect model reasoning to approved tools, datasets, and repeatable workflows.\",\"icon\":\"internal-knowledge\"},{\"text\":\"Compare findings across papers, datasets, and scientific context.\",\"subtitle\":\"Evaluate evidence\",\"icon\":\"new-app\"}]},{\"bestbuy\":\"asdk_app_6a5e8374ba288191a9ee2867002c808e\",\"resy\":\"asdk_app_69c1b187eab4819187448a269e653a74\",\"sams\":\"asdk_app_6a3eb4bdbe688191badec8f7642fa3a9\",\"opentable\":\"asdk_app_6935ba0e15b48191ae8ad5795b213c19\",\"yelp\":\"asdk_app_69e92b0ca8708191a9c3259e162d8e2d\",\"walmart\":\"asdk_app_69b0921773588191a651c86809c15ed7\"},{\"enable_skills_container_upload\":false,\"enable_in_arcade\":false,\"enable_pineapple\":false,\"enabled_example_skills_for_chatgpt\":[],\"enabled_system_skills\":[],\"enable_public_endpoints\":true,\"enable_api\":false,\"container_tool_skill_code_execution_instructions\":\" \",\"enable_separate_pineapple\":false},{\"num_turns\":10},{\"use_singleton_orchestrator_thread\":false,\"version\":\"v3\",\"architecture\":\"avas\",\"model\":\"gpt-live-1.1-codex\",\"include_startup_context\":true,\"recording_enabled\":true,\"use_transceiver_service_discovery\":true},{\"prompt\":\"The user provided feedback on a previous completion.
